*Mohsen Hariri,Michael Hinczewski,Jing Ma,Vipin Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过新发布的 Scorio 库，评估了多种统计排名方法在 LLM 推理模型多次采样评估下的有效性，揭示了不同预算下最可靠的排名策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着推理大模型通过多次采样（test-time scaling）进行评估，如何在高采样量（N > 1）下公平、准确地对模型进行基准测试和排名成为了一个尚未被深入探讨的难题。

Method: 引入 Scorio 库，集成了成对比较模型、项目反应理论 (IRT)、投票规则、基于图和谱的方法等多种统计排名算法。此外，探索了将贪婪解码作为经验先验的贝叶斯优化策略。

Result: 在 AIME 和 HMMT 等数学竞赛基准上，大多数方法与贝叶斯金标准高度一致（Kendall's τ_b > 0.93）；单次采样时，最佳方法可达 0.86；引入贪婪解码先验可在低预算下减少 16-52% 的方差，但可能引入偏差。

Conclusion: Scorio 为 LLM 的测试时缩放（test-time scaling）量化评估提供了规范化的统计框架，证明了在不同采样预算下实现可靠、稳定排名的可行性。

Abstract: Test-time scaling evaluates reasoning LLMs by sampling multiple outputs per prompt, but ranking models in this regime remains underexplored. We formalize dense benchmark ranking under test-time scaling and introduce Scorio, a library that implements statistical ranking methods such as paired-comparison models, item response theory (IRT) models, voting rules, and graph- and spectral-based methods. Across $20$ reasoning models on four Olympiad-style math benchmarks (AIME'24, AIME'25, HMMT'25, and BrUMO'25; up to $N=80$ trials), most full-trial rankings agree closely with the Bayesian gold standard $\mathrm{Bayes}_{\mathcal{U}}@80$ (mean Kendall's $τ_b = 0.93$--$0.95$), and $19$--$34$ methods recover exactly the same ordering. In the single-trial regime, the best methods reach $τ_b \approx 0.86$. Using greedy decoding as an empirical prior ($\mathrm{Bayes}_{\mathbf{R}_0}@N$) reduces variance at $N=1$ by $16$--$52\%$, but can bias rankings when greedy and stochastic sampling disagree. These results identify reliable ranking methods for both high- and low-budget test-time scaling. We release Scorio as an open-source library at https://github.com/mohsenhariri/scorio.

</details>


### [159] [Bio-Inspired Self-Supervised Learning for Wrist-worn IMU Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10961)
*Prithviraj Tarale,Kiet Chu,Abhishek Varghese,Kai-Chun Liu,Maxwell A Xu,Mohit Iyyer,Sunghoon I. Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种模拟人类生物运动结构的 Token 化方法，通过将运动段视为基本单元进行 Transformer 预训练，显著提升了可穿戴传感器活动识别的准确率与数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管自监督学习（SSL）能缓解标注数据稀缺问题，但现有方法将传感器数据视为无结构的序列，忽视了人类运动背后的生物结构特性，限制了表示学习的有效性。

Method: 提出一种基于“子运动理论（Submovement Theory）”的新颖标记化策略。通过将连续手腕运动分解为“运动段（Movement Segments）”作为 Token，利用 Transformer 编码器在大规模数据集（NHANES）上进行掩码运动段重建（Masked Reconstruction）预训练。

Result: 在六个独立受试者的 HAR 基准测试中，该表示显著优于现有的强 SSL 基线模型，并在标注数据稀缺的情况下表现出更强的迁移学习效率。

Conclusion: 将基于生物控制理论（子运动理论）的领域知识引入 SSL 框架，是提升可穿戴设备活动识别性能的一种高效且具有解释性的路径。

Abstract: Wearable accelerometers have enabled large-scale health and wellness monitoring, yet learning robust human-activity representations has been constrained by the scarcity of labeled data. While self-supervised learning offers a potential remedy, existing approaches treat sensor streams as unstructured time series, overlooking the underlying biological structure of human movement, a factor we argue is critical for effective Human Activity Recognition (HAR). We introduce a novel tokenization strategy grounded in the submovement theory of motor control, which posits that continuous wrist motion is composed of superposed elementary basis functions called submovements. We define our token as the movement segment, a unit of motion composed of a finite sequence of submovements that is readily extractable from wrist accelerometer signals. By treating these segments as tokens, we pretrain a Transformer encoder via masked movement-segment reconstruction to model the temporal dependencies of movement segments, shifting the learning focus beyond local waveform morphology. Pretrained on the NHANES corpus (approximately 28k hours; approximately 11k participants; approximately 10M windows), our representations outperform strong wearable SSL baselines across six subject-disjoint HAR benchmarks. Furthermore, they demonstrate stronger data efficiency in data-scarce settings. Code and pretrained weights will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [160] [Factorized Neural Implicit DMD for Parametric Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10995)
*Siyuan Chen,Zhecheng Wang,Yixin Chen,Yue Chang,Peter Yichen Chen,Eitan Grinspun,Jonathan Panuelos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于神经场参数化 Koopman 算子的新模型，通过解耦空间模态和时间演化，实现了对复杂动力学系统稳定、可泛化且具可解释性的长程演化预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统物理系统建模依赖控制方程，且在高维非线性状态下计算昂贵。现有神经场仅能拟合单一解，而神经算子在长程预测和参数泛化方面存在局限。研究旨在寻求一种支持长程预测、参数泛化及频谱分析的模型无关方法。

Method: 提出了一种物理编码神经场（physics-coded neural field）参数化 Koopman 算子的谱分解。该模型通过学习分解后的流算子，将空间模态与时间演化解耦，从而直接通过特征值和模态来表示系统动力学。

Result: 在多种动力学问题上的实验表明，该方法能够精确预测复杂的时空现象，实现稳定的长期演化预测，并支持跨参数空间的插值以及系统稳定性的频谱分析。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种可解释且高效的动力学建模新范式，在保持高预测精度的同时，通过解耦空间和时间信息，为复杂系统的长期演化分析和控制提供了有力工具。

Abstract: A data-driven, model-free approach to modeling the temporal evolution of physical systems mitigates the need for explicit knowledge of the governing equations. Even when physical priors such as partial differential equations are available, such systems often reside in high-dimensional state spaces and exhibit nonlinear dynamics, making traditional numerical solvers computationally expensive and ill-suited for real-time analysis and control. Consider the problem of learning a parametric flow of a dynamical system: with an initial field and a set of physical parameters, we aim to predict the system's evolution over time in a way that supports long-horizon rollouts, generalization to unseen parameters, and spectral analysis.
  We propose a physics-coded neural field parameterization of the Koopman operator's spectral decomposition. Unlike a physics-constrained neural field, which fits a single solution surface, and neural operators, which directly approximate the solution operator at fixed time horizons, our model learns a factorized flow operator that decouples spatial modes and temporal evolution. This structure exposes underlying eigenvalues, modes, and stability of the underlying physical process to enable stable long-term rollouts, interpolation across parameter spaces, and spectral analysis. We demonstrate the efficacy of our method on a range of dynamics problems, showcasing its ability to accurately predict complex spatiotemporal phenomena while providing insights into the system's dynamic behavior.

</details>


### [161] [Cross-Species Transfer Learning for Electrophysiology-to-Transcriptomics Mapping in Cortical GABAergic Interneurons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11000)
*Theo Schwider,Ramin Ramezani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过引入注意力 BiLSTM 模型和跨物种迁移学习，成功复现并扩展了神经元电生理转录组分类框架，提高了在人类数据上的预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证并扩展 Gouwens 等人（2020）提出的电生理-转录组框架，探索如何利用大规模小鼠数据提升对稀缺人类皮层神经元样本（GABA能抑制性中间神经元）的分类预测能力。

Method: 使用标准化电生理特征与稀疏 PCA（sPCA）进行基准测试；开发了基于注意力机制的双向 LSTM（BiLSTM）模型直接处理 IPFX 特征序列；并采用了“小鼠预训练+人类微调”的跨物种迁移学习方案。

Result: 在小鼠数据上复现了主要的细胞类别分离；BiLSTM 模型在无需 sPCA 的情况下达到了与工程特征基线相当的性能；跨物种迁移学习显著提升了人类神经元亚类的宏 F1 分数。

Conclusion: 本研究证实了 Gouwens 等人管道的可重复性，并证明深度学习（尤其是 BiLSTM）与迁移学习相结合，是连接神经元生理特征与转录组身份的有效途径。

Abstract: Single-cell electrophysiological recordings provide a powerful window into neuronal functional diversity and offer an interpretable route for linking intrinsic physiology to transcriptomic identity. Here, we replicate and extend the electrophysiology-to-transcriptomics framework introduced by Gouwens et al. (2020) using publicly available Allen Institute Patch-seq datasets from both mouse and human cortex. We focus on GABAergic inhibitory interneurons to target a subclass structure (Lamp5, Pvalb, Sst, Vip) that is comparable and conserved across species. After quality control, we analyzed 3,699 mouse visual cortex neurons and 506 human neocortical neurons from neurosurgical resections. Using standardized electrophysiological features and sparse PCA, we reproduced the major class-level separations reported in the original mouse study. For supervised prediction, a class-balanced random forest provided a strong feature-engineered baseline in mouse data and a reduced but still informative baseline in human data. We then developed an attention-based BiLSTM that operates directly on the structured IPFX feature-family representation, avoiding sPCA and providing feature-family-level interpretability via learned attention weights. Finally, we evaluated a cross-species transfer setting in which the sequence model is pretrained on mouse data and fine-tuned on human data for an aligned 4-class task, improving human macro-F1 relative to a human-only training baseline. Together, these results confirm reproducibility of the Gouwens pipeline in mouse data, demonstrate that sequence models can match feature-engineered baselines, and show that mouse-to-human transfer learning can provide measurable gains for human subclass prediction.

</details>


### [162] [Leech Lattice Vector Quantization for Efficient LLM Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11021)
*Tycho F. A. van der Ouderaa,Mart van Baalen,Paul Whatmough,Markus Nagel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 LLVQ，利用 24 维 Leech 格点的最优堆积特性实现大模型向量量化，在无需显式码本存储的情况下取得了最先进的压缩效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标量量化受限于信息论极限，而向量量化（VQ）虽能突破限制，但传统 VQ 依赖昂贵的查找表。现有格点量化缺乏在极高维度（如 24 维）下的高效实现方案。

Method: 提出 Leech 格点向量量化（LLVQ）。通过扩展基于 Golay 码的搜索算法，实现了高效索引（无需存储码本）、支持多层格点壳层的角度搜索，并开发了全并行的反量化内核。

Result: LLVQ 在 LLM 量化性能上达到了 SOTA 水准，超越了 Quip#、QTIP 和 PVQ 等现有主流方法。

Conclusion: 高维格点（尤其是 Leech 格点）是实现可扩展且具有理论基础的大模型压缩的关键方向。

Abstract: Scalar quantization of large language models (LLMs) is fundamentally limited by information-theoretic bounds. While vector quantization (VQ) overcomes these limits by encoding blocks of parameters jointly, practical implementations must avoid the need for expensive lookup mechanisms or other explicit codebook storage. Lattice approaches address this through highly structured and dense packing. This paper explores the Leech lattice, which, with its optimal sphere packing and kissing configurations at 24 dimensions, is the highest dimensional lattice known with such optimal properties. To make the Leech lattice usable for LLM quantization, we extend an existing search algorithm based on the extended Golay code construction, to i) support indexing, enabling conversion to and from bitstrings without materializing the codebook, ii) allow angular search over union of Leech lattice shells, iii) propose fully-parallelisable dequantization kernel. Together this yields a practical algorithm, namely Leech Lattice Vector Quantization (LLVQ). LLVQ delivers state-of-the-art LLM quantization performance, outperforming recent methods such as Quip\#, QTIP, and PVQ. These results highlight the importance of high-dimensional lattices for scalable, theoretically grounded model compression.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [163] [LLMGreenRec: LLM-Based Multi-Agent Recommender System for Sustainable E-Commerce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11025)
*Hao N. Nguyen,Hieu M. Nguyen,Son Van Nguyen,Nguyen Thi Hanh*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出 LLMGreenRec 框架，利用多智能体 LLM 捕捉用户环保意图，在提升绿色产品推荐效果的同时降低了系统自身的数字碳足迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管电子商务领域的环保意识不断增强，但传统的会话式推荐系统仍侧重于短期转化，难以捕捉用户的可持续消费意图，且其自身的数字碳足迹也需要优化。

Method: 提出了 LLMGreenRec，这是一个多智能体（Multi-agent）框架。该框架利用大语言模型（LLMs）通过协作分析用户行为和迭代提示词优化，推断以绿色为导向的用户意图并优先推荐环保产品。

Result: 在基准数据集上的实验证明，LLMGreenRec 能有效推荐可持续产品，并由于其意图驱动的方法减少了不必要的交互，从而降低了能源消耗。

Conclusion: LLMGreenRec 为电子商务提供了一种稳健的解决方案，在促进负责任的数字经济的同时，缩小了消费者绿色意图与行动之间的差距。

Abstract: Rising environmental awareness in e-commerce necessitates recommender systems that not only guide users to sustainable products but also minimize their own digital carbon footprints. Traditional session-based systems, optimized for short-term conversions, often fail to capture nuanced user intents for eco-friendly choices, perpetuating a gap between green intentions and actions. To tackle this, we introduce LLMGreenRec, a novel multi-agent framework that leverages Large Language Models (LLMs) to promote sustainable consumption. Through collaborative analysis of user interactions and iterative prompt refinement, LLMGreenRec's specialized agents deduce green-oriented user intents and prioritize eco-friendly product recommendations. Notably, this intent-driven approach also reduces unnecessary interactions and energy consumption. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets validate LLMGreenRec's effectiveness in recommending sustainable products, demonstrating a robust solution that fosters a responsible digital economy.

</details>


<div id='cs.GT'></div>

# cs.GT [[Back]](#toc)

### [164] [Quantal Response Equilibrium as a Measure of Strategic Sophistication: Theory and Validation for LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10029)
*Mateo Pechon-Elkins,Jon Chun*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于博弈论QRE的LLM评估框架，通过定量分析理性参数发现，虽然前沿模型展现了一定的战略推理能力，但在稳定性和认知深度上仍与人类有显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM心智理论（ToM）基准测试缺乏理论基础，仅提供聚合分数，无法区分模型是具备战略推理能力还是仅依赖表面启发式搜索。

Method: 引入基于量子反应均衡（QRE）的博弈论评估框架，推导出四种不同认知能力博弈的闭式解，并通过估计理性参数 $\lambda$ 为模型表现打分，同时利用鞅分布建立有限样本收敛界。

Result: 验证显示模型诈唬率接近均衡（4%以内），但理性参数 $\lambda$（0.05-1.10）普遍低于人类水平（1.0-2.5）；不同模型在认知维度上表现各异，且极易受提示词框架影响。

Conclusion: QRE框架为LLM的心智理论评估提供了严谨的数学基础。研究发现模型虽能通过博弈论验证，但其行为依然表现出高度的变体敏感性和版本不稳定性。

Abstract: Theory of Mind benchmarks for large language models typically produce aggregate scores without theoretical grounding, making it unclear whether high performance reflects strategic reasoning or surface-level heuristics. We introduce a game-theoretic evaluation framework grounded in quantal response equilibrium (QRE). We derive closed-form equilibria for four strategic games, each targeting a distinct cognitive capability. We estimate QRE rationality parameters lambda that place model behavior on a continuous scale calibrated against human data (lambda_human in [1.0, 2.5]), and establish finite-sample convergence bounds via martingale concentration. Validation across 1,855 games with seven frontier models (plus four expansion models) confirms predictions: bluff rates converge to within 4% of equilibrium, lambda estimates range from 0.05 to 1.10 across games and models with substantial cross-model variation, and capability profiles differ across cognitive axes. Robustness analyses reveal high sensitivity to prompt framing and version instability in QRE rankings, highlighting the need for standardized protocols.

</details>


### [165] [Instant Runoff Voting on Graphs: Exclusion Zones and Distortion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10290)
*Georgios Birmpas,Georgios Chionas,Efthyvoulos Drousiotis,Soodeh Habibi,Marios Mavronicolas,Paul Spirakis*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了图度量空间下的IRV投票规则，证明了在树上寻找候选人“排除区”具有多项式算法，而在一般图中则是计算困难的，并分析了该规则的效用失真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在图度量空间下的即时决选制（IRV）及其“排除区（Exclusion Zones）”问题。即探讨在给定的图中，候选人分布在哪些区域会导致获胜者必然出现在该区域内，并分析其计算复杂度和度量失真。

Method: 通过设计“Kill成员测试”并利用自底向上的动态规划算法，在树上判定特定候选人是否可被淘汰。同时，利用贪心收缩过程寻找最小排除区。针对非树结构，通过定义“强迫淘汰（Strong Forced Elimination）”属性进行复杂度分析。

Result: 证明了在一般图中测试排除区是co-NP完全的，寻找最小排除区是NP难的；但在树状图中，两者均可在多项式时间内完成。此外，确定了一类满足强迫淘汰属性的规则在计算上均是困难的，并给出了IRV在二叉树和一般图中的效用失真上下界。

Conclusion: 在树状图这一特定结构下，排除区问题是可解的，但在更广泛的确定性排名消除规则或一般图结构下，往往面临计算复杂度上的挑战。此外，该研究将计算选择理论与度量失真分析相结合，深化了对IRV性能的理解。

Abstract: We study instant-runoff voting (IRV) under metric preferences induced by an unweighted graph where each vertex hosts a voter, candidates occupy some vertices (with a single candidate allowed in such a vertex), and voters rank candidates by shortest-path distance with fixed deterministic tie-breaking. We focus on exclusion zones, vertex sets S such that whenever some candidate lies in S, the IRV winner must also lie in S. While testing whether a given set S is an exclusion zone is co-NP-Complete and finding the minimum exclusion zone is NP-hard in general graphs, we show here that both problems can be solved in polynomial time on trees. Our approach solves zone testing by designing a Kill membership test (can a designated candidate be forced to lose using opponents from a restricted set?) and shows that Kill can be decided in polynomial time on trees via a bottom-up dynamic program that certifies whether the designated candidate can be eliminated in round 1. A greedy shrinking process then recovers the minimum zone under a standard nesting assumption. To clarify the limits of tractability beyond trees, we also identify a rule level property (Strong Forced Elimination) that abstracts the key IRV behavior used in prior reductions, and show that both exclusion-zone verification and minimum- zone computation remain co-NP-complete and NP-hard, respectively, for any deterministic rank-based elimination rule satisfying this property. Finally, we relate IRV to utilitarian distortion in this discrete setting, and we present upper and lower bounds with regard to the distortion of IRV for several scenarios, including perfect binary trees and unweighted graphs.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [166] [A Diffusion Analysis of Policy Gradient for Stochastic Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10219)
*Tor Lattimore*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文利用扩散近似分析了策略梯度在 $k$ 臂老虎机中的表现，揭示了学习率 $\eta$ 必须足够小（与差距 $\Delta$ 的平方成比例）才能保证对数级遗憾度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在通过理论分析明确学习率 $\eta$ 与算法遗憾度（Regret）之间的对应关系，解决策略梯度在随机老虎机问题中的收敛性与遗憾界限问题。

Method: 采用连续时间扩散近似（Continuous-time Diffusion Approximation）方法来研究随机 $k$ 臂老虎机的策略梯度算法。

Result: 证明了当学习率 $\eta = O(\Delta^2/\log(n))$ 时，遗憾度为 $O(k \log(k) \log(n) / \eta)$；同时构造了一个特例证明，若学习率不满足 $\eta = O(\Delta^2)$，即使臂的数量很少，遗憾度也会变成线性。

Conclusion: 在 $k$ 臂老虎机问题中，策略梯度算法的遗憾度下界与学习率 $\eta$ 密切相关，且 $\eta$ 的设置存在必要的阈值限制以保证算法不失效。

Abstract: We study a continuous-time diffusion approximation of policy gradient for $k$-armed stochastic bandits. We prove that with a learning rate $η= O(Δ^2/\log(n))$ the regret is $O(k \log(k) \log(n) / η)$ where $n$ is the horizon and $Δ$ the minimum gap. Moreover, we construct an instance with only logarithmically many arms for which the regret is linear unless $η= O(Δ^2)$.

</details>


### [167] [Bayesian Hierarchical Models and the Maximum Entropy Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10252)
*Brendon J. Brewer*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文分析了贝叶斯层次模型，证明了若给定超参数的先验是最大熵分布，则积分后的边缘先验也具备最大熵特性，从而揭示了层次模型隐含的信息约束机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探讨贝叶斯层次模型中边缘先验（Marginal Prior）的内在属性，理解通过引入超参数所建立的参数间依赖性在信息论层面意味着什么。

Method: 通过数学推导，研究了当超参数给定的先验分布为正则分布（具有矩约束的最大熵分布）时，计算其积分掉超参数后的边缘先验分布的性质。

Result: 证明了在这种情况下，生成的依赖型边缘先验同样具有最大熵性质，但其约束条件转移到了未知量的某个特定函数的边缘分布上。

Conclusion: 该结论阐明了当我们分配层次模型时，实际上是在对未知量的某些函数的边缘分布进行约束，揭示了层次先验中隐含的信息结构。

Abstract: Bayesian hierarchical models are frequently used in practical data analysis contexts. One interpretation of these models is that they provide an indirect way of assigning a prior for unknown parameters, through the introduction of hyperparameters. The resulting marginal prior for the parameters (integrating over the hyperparameters) is usually dependent, so that learning one parameter provides some information about the others. In this contribution, I will demonstrate that, when the prior given the hyperparameters is a canonical distribution (a maximum entropy distribution with moment constraints), the dependent marginal prior also has a maximum entropy property, with a different constraint. This constraint is on the marginal distribution of some function of the unknown quantities. The results shed light on what information is actually being assumed when we assign a hierarchical model.

</details>


### [168] [MultiwayPAM: Multiway Partitioning Around Medoids for LLM-as-a-Judge Score Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10287)
*Chihiro Watanabe,Jingyu Sun*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出 MultiwayPAM 张量聚类方法，旨在通过分析“问题-回答者-评估者”三个维度的评分张量，解决 LLM 作为评估者时的计算开销与评分偏差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-Judge 框架面临计算成本高昂以及 LLM 评估者固有偏差的挑战。

Method: 开发了一种名为 MultiwayPAM 的新型张量聚类方法，能够同时估计数据张量每个维度的聚类成员身份和中心点（medoids）。

Result: 在两个实际数据集上的实验验证了该方法的有效性，能够通过观察中心点来识别问题、回答者和评估者的聚类特征。

Conclusion: MultiwayPAM 是揭示 LLM 评分偏差结构并降低大规模评估成本的有效工具。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-Judge is a flexible framework for text evaluation, which allows us to obtain scores for the quality of a given text from various perspectives by changing the prompt template. Two main challenges in using LLM-as-a-Judge are computational cost of LLM inference, especially when evaluating a large number of texts, and inherent bias of an LLM evaluator. To address these issues and reveal the structure of score bias caused by an LLM evaluator, we propose to apply a tensor clustering method to a given LLM-as-a-Judge score tensor, whose entries are the scores for different combinations of questions, answerers, and evaluators. Specifically, we develop a new tensor clustering method MultiwayPAM, with which we can simultaneously estimate the cluster membership and the medoids for each mode of a given data tensor. By observing the medoids obtained by MultiwayPAM, we can gain knowledge about the membership of each question/answerer/evaluator cluster. We experimentally show the effectiveness of MultiwayPAM by applying it to the score tensors for two practical datasets.

</details>


### [169] [On The Complexity of Best-Arm Identification in Non-Stationary Linear Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10346)
*Leo Maynard-Zhang,Zhihan Xiong,Kevin Jamieson,Maryam Fazel*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了非平稳线性老虎机的固定预算最佳臂识别问题，提出了首个与臂集几何结构相关的紧致下界，并开发了达到该复杂度的最优采样算法Adjacent-BAI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的G-最优设计采样方法虽然在极小极大（minimax）意义下最优，但其复杂性指标$H_G$过于保守，无法反映具有丰富几何特征的臂集的优势。研究者旨在寻求一个能体现具体臂集结构的紧致下界及相应算法。

Method: 提出了基于“相邻最优设计”（Adjacent-optimal design）的Adjacent-BAI算法。该设计是XY-最优设计的特化，通过优化采样策略来最小化识别累积奖励最大臂的误差。

Result: 建立了一个适用于任何臂集的下界。证明了Adjacent-BAI算法的误差概率在常数范围内匹配该下界，克服了传统G-最优设计中复杂度仅由维度$d$决定的局限性。

Conclusion: 本研究确立了非平稳线性多臂老虎机在固定预算设置下的臂集依赖型复杂度，并证明了Adjacent-BAI算法在该设置下的最优性。

Abstract: We study the fixed-budget best-arm identification (BAI) problem in non-stationary linear bandits. Concretely, given a fixed time budget $T\in \mathbb{N}$, finite arm set $\mathcal{X} \subset \mathbb{R}^d$, and a potentially adversarial sequence of unknown parameters $\lbrace θ_t\rbrace_{t=1}^{T}$ (hence non-stationary), a learner aims to identify the arm with the largest cumulative reward $x_* = \arg\max_{x \in \mathcal{X}} x^\top\sum_{t=1}^T θ_t$ with high probability. In this setting, it is well-known that uniformly sampling arms from the G-optimal design yields a minimax-optimal error probability of $\exp\left(-Θ\left(T / H_{G}\right)\right)$, where $H_{G}$ scales proportionally with the dimension $d$. However, this notion of complexity is overly pessimistic, as it is derived from a lower bound in which the arm set consists only of the standard basis vectors, thus masking any potential advantages arising from arm sets with richer geometric structure. To address this, we establish an arm-set-dependent lower bound that, in contrast, holds for any arm set. Motivated by the ideas underlying our lower bound, we propose the Adjacent-optimal design, a specialization of the well-known $\mathcal{X}\mathcal{Y}$-optimal design, and develop the $\textsf{Adjacent-BAI}$ algorithm. We prove that the error probability of $\textsf{Adjacent-BAI}$ matches our lower bound up to constants, verifying the tightness of our lower bound, and establishing the arm-set-dependent complexity of this setting.

</details>


### [170] [Adaptive Active Learning for Regression via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10435)
*Simon D. Nguyen,Troy Russo,Kentaro Hoffman,Tyler H. McCormick*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出 WiGS 方法，利用强化学习动态调整采样策略的权重，显著提升了回归任务中主动学习的样本选择效率和模型精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的改进贪婪采样（iGS）方法使用固定的乘法规则平衡特征空间和输出空间，导致在数据密度不均时会忽略高密度区域的高误差样本。

Method: 提出加权改进贪婪采样（WiGS），将采样准则从“静态乘法”改为“动态加法”，并引入深度强化学习（DRL）作为智能体，根据学习进度动态调整特征多样性与模型不确定性之间的权重。

Result: 在 18 个基准数据集和合成环境上的实验表明，WiGS 在准确性和标注效率方面均优于 iGS 及其他基准方法。

Conclusion: WiGS 成功解决了传统 iGS 在处理分布不均数据时的局限性，通过 DR-RL 框架实现了主动学习过程中重心的动态自适应，是回归任务主动学习领域的一大进步。

Abstract: Active learning for regression reduces labeling costs by selecting the most informative samples. Improved Greedy Sampling is a prominent method that balances feature-space diversity and output-space uncertainty using a static, multiplicative rule. We propose Weighted improved Greedy Sampling (WiGS), which replaces this framework with a dynamic, additive criterion. We formulate weight selection as a reinforcement learning problem, enabling an agent to adapt the exploration-investigation balance throughout learning. Experiments on 18 benchmark datasets and a synthetic environment show WiGS outperforms iGS and other baseline methods in both accuracy and labeling efficiency, particularly in domains with irregular data density where the baseline's multiplicative rule ignores high-error samples in dense regions.

</details>


### [171] [Brenier Isotonic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10452)
*Han Bao,Amirreza Eshraghi,Yutong Wang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文通过最优传输理论将保序回归扩展到多输出场景，提出“Brenier 保序回归”，解决了高维单调性约束难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的保序回归（IR）仅适用于单变量非递减函数，难以直接扩展到多输出回归场景，因为高维空间中的单调性定义不直观且难以强制执行。

Method: 利用 Kantorovich 最优传输（OT）理论，将回归函数视为梯度场（即 Brenier 势能的梯度），通过强制实现“循环单调性”（Cyclic Monotonicity）来构建多输出保序回归。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在概率校准（Probability Calibration）和广义线性模型中表现优异，尤其在稳健性方面显著超过了现有的主流基准算法。

Conclusion: Brenier 保序回归通过最优传输理论成功解决了多输出回归中的单调性扩展问题，在概率校准等任务中展现出优于传统基准模型的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Isotonic regression (IR) is shape-constrained regression to maintain a univariate fitting curve non-decreasing, which has numerous applications including single-index models and probability calibration. When it comes to multi-output regression, the classical IR is no longer applicable because the monotonicity is not readily extendable. We consider a novel multi-output regression problem where a regression function is \emph{cyclically monotone}. Roughly speaking, a cyclically monotone function is the gradient of some convex potential. Whereas enforcing cyclic monotonicity is apparently challenging, we leverage the fact that Kantorovich's optimal transport (OT) always yields a cyclically monotone coupling as an optimal solution. This perspective naturally allows us to interpret a regression function and the convex potential as a link function in generalized linear models and Brenier's potential in OT, respectively, and hence we call this IR extension \emph{Brenier isotonic regression}. We demonstrate experiments with probability calibration and generalized linear models. In particular, IR outperforms many famous baselines in probability calibration robustly.

</details>


### [172] [Dual Space Preconditioning for Gradient Descent in the Overparameterized Regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10485)
*Reza Ghane,Danil Akhtiamov,Babak Hassibi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文证明了双空间预训练梯度下降在线性超参数化模型中的收敛性，并揭示了其隐式偏差与梯度下降（GD）之间的紧密联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究双空间预训练梯度下降（如 Adam、梯度裁剪等）在大模型训练（超参数化线性模型）中的收敛性质及其隐式偏差。

Method: 引入了一种新型的 Bregman 散度及其相关恒等式来证明收敛性，并对各向同性和一般预处理器的隐式偏差进行了理论与实证分析。

Result: 证明了算法在满足条件下必收敛于全局解。发现隐式偏差通常取决于学习率，但各向同性预处理器的隐式偏差与普通 GD 相同，且一般预处理器的解与 GD 解存在范数约束关系。

Conclusion: 在超参数化线性模型中，DSPGD 始终收敛。对于各向同性预处理器，其隐式偏差与普通梯度下降一致；对于一般预处理器，其解在范数意义上与 GD 解保持相近。

Abstract: In this work we study the convergence properties of the Dual Space Preconditioned Gradient Descent, encompassing optimizers such as Normalized Gradient Descent, Gradient Clipping and Adam. We consider preconditioners of the form $\nabla K$, where $K: \mathbb{R}^p \to \mathbb{R}$ is convex and assume that the latter is applied to train an over-parameterized linear model with loss of the form $\ell({X} {W} - {Y})$, for weights ${W} \in \mathbb{R}^{d \times k}$, labels ${Y} \in \mathbb{R}^{n \times k}$ and data ${X} \in \mathbb{R}^{n \times d}$. Under the aforementioned assumptions, we prove that the iterates of the preconditioned gradient descent always converge to a point ${W}_{\infty} \in \mathbb{R}^{d \times k}$ satisfying ${X}{W}_{\infty} = {Y}$. Our proof techniques are of independent interest as we introduce a novel version of the Bregman Divergence with accompanying identities that allow us to establish convergence. We also study the implicit bias of Dual Space Preconditioned Gradient Descent. First, we demonstrate empirically that, for general $K(\cdot)$, ${W}_\infty$ depends on the chosen learning rate, hindering a precise characterization of the implicit bias. Then, for preconditioners of the form $K({G}) = h(\|{G}\|_F)$, known as \textit{isotropic preconditioners}, we show that ${W}_\infty$ minimizes $\|{W}_\infty - {W}_0\|_F^2$ subject to ${X}{W}_\infty = {Y}$, where ${W}_0$ is the initialization. Denoting the convergence point of GD initialized at ${W}_0$ by ${W}_{\text{GD}, \infty}$, we thus note ${W}_{\infty} = {W}_{\text{GD}, \infty}$ for isotropic preconditioners. Finally, we show that a similar fact holds for general preconditioners up to a multiplicative constant, namely, $\|{W}_0 - {W}_{\infty}\|_F \le c \|{W}_0 - {W}_{\text{GD}, \infty}\|_F$ for a constant $c>0$.

</details>


### [173] [ReTabSyn: Realistic Tabular Data Synthesis via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10823)
*Xiaofeng Lin,Seungbae Kim,Zhuoya Li,Zachary DeSoto,Charles Fleming,Guang Cheng*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: ReTabSyn 通过强化学习优化特征相关性的保留，提高了小样本和不平衡场景下合成表格数据的下游模型效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深层生成模型在处理小样本和类别不平衡的表格数据时，难以完全捕获复杂的联合分布，导致生成的合成数据实用性受限。

Method: 提出了 ReTabSyn 框架，利用强化学习（Reinforced Tabular Synthesis）在训练过程中提供关于特征相关性保留的直接反馈，并基于大语言模型（LM）生成器进行微调。

Result: 在小样本、类别不平衡和分布偏移的基准测试中，ReTabSyn 一致优于现有最先进（SOTA）方法，并能灵活支持专家定义的约束条件。

Conclusion: 在训练合成器时直接引入特征相关性反馈，是提升生成数据质量的一种比追求完整联合分布更有效的策略。

Abstract: Deep generative models can help with data scarcity and privacy by producing synthetic training data, but they struggle in low-data, imbalanced tabular settings to fully learn the complex data distribution. We argue that striving for the full joint distribution could be overkill; for greater data efficiency, models should prioritize learning the conditional distribution $P(y\mid \bm{X})$, as suggested by recent theoretical analysis. Therefore, we overcome this limitation with \textbf{ReTabSyn}, a \textbf{Re}inforced \textbf{Tab}ular \textbf{Syn}thesis pipeline that provides direct feedback on feature correlation preservation during synthesizer training. This objective encourages the generator to prioritize the most useful predictive signals when training data is limited, thereby strengthening downstream model utility. We empirically fine-tune a language model-based generator using this approach, and across benchmarks with small sample sizes, class imbalance, and distribution shift, ReTabSyn consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. Moreover, our approach can be readily extended to control various aspects of synthetic tabular data, such as applying expert-specified constraints on generated observations.

</details>


### [174] [Kernel Tests of Equivalence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10886)
*Xing Liu,Axel Gandy*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套基于核方法的非参数等价性检验框架，利用KSD和MMD来量化分布间差异，解决了传统统计检验无法严谨证明分布“无显着差异”的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的拟合优度检验由于可能因样本量不足产生二类错误（Type-II error），无法直接证明两个分布等价；且现有等价性检验多局限于参数分布或特定矩，无法覆盖全分布。

Method: 基于核斯坦散度（KSD）和最大均值差异（MMD）构建统计量，假设零假设为两分布间差异大于预设阈值。通过渐近正态近似和自助法（Bootstrap）计算临界值。

Result: 实验证明，该方法能有效控制第一类错误率，并在复杂的非参数设定下表现出良好的检测能力。

Conclusion: 核等价性检验能够有效评估分布间的细微差异，为科学研究中“证明无差异”的需求提供了可靠的数学工具。

Abstract: We propose novel kernel-based tests for assessing the equivalence between distributions. Traditional goodness-of-fit testing is inappropriate for concluding the absence of distributional differences, because failure to reject the null hypothesis may simply be a result of lack of test power, also known as the Type-II error. This motivates \emph{equivalence testing}, which aims to assess the \emph{absence} of a statistically meaningful effect under controlled error rates. However, existing equivalence tests are either limited to parametric distributions or focus only on specific moments rather than the full distribution. We address these limitations using two kernel-based statistical discrepancies: the \emph{kernel Stein discrepancy} and the \emph{Maximum Mean Discrepancy}. The null hypothesis of our proposed tests assumes the candidate distribution differs from the nominal distribution by at least a pre-defined margin, which is measured by these discrepancies. We propose two approaches for computing the critical values of the tests, one using an asymptotic normality approximation, and another based on bootstrapping. Numerical experiments are conducted to assess the performance of these tests.

</details>
